# SRD 5.2.1 early-game monsters (CR 0 to 30, all 330): the route test

Written by `EARLY_ROUTES_OUT=$PWD/docs/SRD_EARLY_GAME_ROUTES.md go test ./cmd/server -run TestPostgresEarlyGameEveryEntry` (srd_early_game_routes_2024_test.go, Postgres): one run's result. Every action, Bonus Action and Reaction (and Legendary Action) of the 330 monsters sent through POST /api/gm/narrate's monster_action at a character, once per option its entry offers (`use`), after the setup its gate needs; an attack repeated until it hits and a save until it fails. "Stored" is what the server wrote; "flagged to the GM" means the reply hands the GM what the server doesn't do. Movement is run without a battle map here (the GM places the creature). Traits: who applies each, as the GM's view of the fight says.

Runs: 1293 over 989 actions, Bonus Actions and Reactions; traits: 329. Verdicts: map[applied:765 applied, rest flagged to the GM:286 flagged to the GM:242].
